### 2026-04-19 — Status-aware resume demotion: FAILED envelopes re-execute their stage (Session 52)

Fixes BACKLOG item 1 (filed by Session 51's live-LLM round-trip, `run_id=run_b1_resume_live_1776570556`). `determine_resume_point` previously used only `CheckpointStore.has()` existence checks, so a `DataReport.json` on disk with `status=EXECUTION_FAILED` — the halt artifact of a `FAILED_AT_DATA` run — was treated as a completed handoff and the resume point returned `"website"`, handing the FAILED report to the website agent. The symmetric bug existed for `IntakeReport` with `status=DRAFT_INCOMPLETE`. Session 52's fix: `determine_resume_point` loads saved `IntakeReport` / `DataReport` payloads and demotes the resume point to `"intake"` / `"data"` when the saved status is not `"COMPLETE"`. `ResumeInconsistent` contract stays narrow (missing-predecessor cases only) — a FAILED envelope is a legitimate halt artifact, not structural corruption. `RepoProjectResult` is NOT symmetric: its FAILED case is handled by `_handle_already_complete` with opt-in-to-retry UX because the website stage has irreversible side effects (GitLab/GitHub project creation).

- **Changed:** `src/model_project_constructor/orchestrator/pipeline.py` — `determine_resume_point` restructured so that (a) `RepoProjectResult` existence short-circuits before any payload load (keeps the hot path free of deserialization when a terminal result is present; preserves Session 51 CLI tests that seed stub `{"status": "COMPLETE"}` envelopes), (b) `IntakeReport` / `DataReport` status is loaded and checked only at the branch points where it materially affects the resume decision, (c) a non-`COMPLETE` saved payload demotes to the re-execution point for that stage. Added `_is_saved_payload_complete(store, run_id, payload_type)` private helper. Docstring updated to cross-reference plan §11 risk #8 and explain the asymmetry with `RepoProjectResult`.
- **Changed:** `tests/orchestrator/test_pipeline.py` — migrated S1–S5 tests in `TestDetermineResumePoint` from `_touch_checkpoint` (writes empty `{}` JSON) to `_seed_envelope` (writes real `HandoffEnvelope` with valid payloads), because the pure-function tests now exercise a path that loads the payload to check status. S0 and INVALID A/B/C tests stay on `_touch_checkpoint` (they fire before any load attempt). Helpers `_incomplete_data_report` / `_draft_incomplete_intake` pre-existed from `TestHaltBehavior` — reused rather than duplicated.
- **Added:** two regression tests in `TestDetermineResumePoint` — `test_failed_data_report_demotes_to_data` (seeds `IntakeReport` COMPLETE + `DataRequest` + `DataReport` with `status=EXECUTION_FAILED` → asserts resume point is `"data"` not `"website"`) and `test_draft_incomplete_intake_demotes_to_intake` (seeds `IntakeReport` with `status=DRAFT_INCOMPLETE` alone → asserts `"intake"`). Each test cites `run_id=run_b1_resume_live_1776570556` as the original production evidence so future readers can trace the fix to its discovery.
- **Changed:** `docs/planning/resume-from-checkpoint-plan.md` §11 — added risk #8 ("the inverse of #5") documenting the bug, its observation in Session 51's live-LLM round-trip, and the Session 52 fix including the `RepoProjectResult` asymmetry rationale and the `ResumeInconsistent` contract narrowing.
- **Removed:** one BACKLOG.md line per Learning #26 (delete, do NOT flip to `[x]`) — "Resume of FAILED data stage skips re-execution" (now fixed). BACKLOG net change: 7 `[ ]` → 6 `[ ]`, 0 `[x]` → 0 `[x]`.
- **Verified:** `uv run pytest -q` → **470/470 passing** (was 468 pre-session; +2 new regression tests, -0 test removals). Coverage **97.27%** with `Required test coverage of 95% reached` — floor holds (2.27-point headroom; 0.06 drop from Session 51's 97.33 is the new `_is_saved_payload_complete` helper's defensive branch for the impossible-under-normal-run-semantics `request_present` + non-`COMPLETE` intake case). `uv run ruff check src/ tests/ packages/` → `All checks passed!`. `uv run mypy src/` → `Success: no issues found in 48 source files`. `orchestrator/pipeline.py` coverage: 100% on 98 statements + 32 branches (was 92 stmts + 32 branches pre-session).
- **Unchanged intentionally:** `CheckpointStore` API surface untouched. `run_pipeline` execution path untouched (Session 50's Phase 2 wiring still honors `resume_from` as before; status-awareness is entirely inside `determine_resume_point`). ### 2026-04-18 — Resume-from-checkpoint Phase 3: `--resume` CLI flag + OPERATIONS + tutorial (Session 51)

Closes the resume-from-checkpoint plan's critical path (Phases 1–3) per `docs/planning/resume-from-checkpoint-plan.md` §7.3 + §14. `scripts/run_pipeline.py --resume <run_id>` resolves the resume point via `determine_resume_point` (Phase 1) and threads it into `PipelineConfig.resume_from` (Phase 2); `run_pipeline` then loads every envelope before the resume point and re-executes from that point onward. Operator-facing exit codes/messages match plan §7.3.2 verbatim. BACKLOG loses two lines — the "Automated resume-from-checkpoint" item (now shipped) and the "B-3 Web UI bridge" item (per §1.3 supersession: once `--resume` exists, the UI → pipeline handoff becomes "UI writes `IntakeReport.json` envelope, operator invokes `--resume`," with no bespoke `--resume-intake` flag needed). Phase 4 (UI writes the envelope) remains plan-§7.4 OPTIONAL and is deferred unless a pilot operator demands the UI-driven workflow.

- **Added:** `scripts/run_pipeline.py` — `--resume <run_id>` argparse flag (overrides `--run-id` per plan §11 risk #3); three inline helpers (`_resume_preflight` for the S0 "no checkpoints" rejection, `_handle_already_complete` for the S5 COMPLETE/FAILED branching that reads the saved `RepoProjectResult.result.json` to distinguish, `_resolve_resume` orchestrating both + `ResumeInconsistent` translation); `_SKIPPED_STAGES_BY_RESUME_POINT` mapping for the "Skipping: ..." banner. The resumed `run_id` overrides the auto-generated one before the banner prints, so the operator-facing log shows the actual run being continued. Imports `CheckpointStore`, `ResumeInconsistent`, `ResumePoint`, `determine_resume_point` from the orchestrator package (all three are public re-exports landed by Sessions 49/50).
- **Added:** `tests/scripts/test_run_pipeline_resume.py` — 7 tests covering argparse acceptance, `_resume_preflight` rejection (exit 2 + "no checkpoints" stderr message), `ResumeInconsistent` translation (DataReport without DataRequest), `already_complete` COMPLETE → exit 0 with `project_url`, `already_complete` FAILED → exit 2 with retry recipe naming the result-file path, `_resolve_resume` returns "data" for an S2-seeded checkpoint dir, end-to-end `main()` integration that monkeypatches `run_pipeline` to capture the constructed `PipelineConfig` and asserts `config.resume_from == "data"` + `config.run_id == <resumed id>` + the resume banner ("RESUMED from: data" + "Skipping: intake, intake_to_data_adapter") prints to stdout. Loaded via the existing `importlib.util` pattern from `test_run_pipeline_adapter.py` (no package promotion).

Start a new run without --resume.` Synthetic round-trip captured: `--run-id resume_smoke_synth --checkpoint-dir /tmp/mpc_resume_smoke` (fake mode) → COMPLETE; second `--resume resume_smoke_synth` on the COMPLETE run → exit 0 + "already complete" message + project URL `https://fake.host.test/data-science/model-drafts/subrogation-pilot`; delete `RepoProjectResult.result.json` then re-`--resume` → banner shows "RESUMED from: website / Skipping: intake, intake_to_data_adapter, data" and the website stage runs alone → COMPLETE.

LangGraph intra-agent checkpointers (`MemorySaver`/`SqliteSaver`) untouched per plan §10 anti-scope. Plan's Phase 4 (UI writes `IntakeReport.json` envelope) NOT implemented — remains §7.4 OPTIONAL.
- **Live-LLM round-trip captured (Session 51 addendum, 2026-04-18):** Operator authorized a live B1 round-trip post-commit. Run #1: `uv run python scripts/run_pipeline.py --live --host gitlab --llm data --model claude-sonnet-4-6 --db-url sqlite:///does-not-exist.db --run-id run_b1_resume_live_1776570556` halted at `FAILED_AT_DATA` after 58 s of data-agent time as plan §13 predicted, saving `IntakeReport.json` + `DataRequest.json` + `DataReport.json` (with `status=EXECUTION_FAILED`). Cost ~$0.05–0.10. Run #2 (`--resume`) was NOT executed: pre-resume inspection of the saved `DataReport.json` surfaced two bugs (filed as new BACKLOG items, see below) that would have caused the resume to either crash or generate a broken GitLab project; Session 51 stopped the round-trip and filed the findings rather than burn additional LLM cost on already-known-broken paths. The synthetic fake-mode round-trip remains the end-to-end exit-code proof.
- **Findings from the live round-trip — filed to BACKLOG:** (1) `determine_resume_point` returns `"website"` whenever `DataReport.json` exists on disk, regardless of the saved report's `status`. A run that halted at `FAILED_AT_DATA` therefore resumes by *skipping* the data stage and handing the FAILED report to the website agent — the mirror image of plan §11 risk #5, which named the "loaded-data wrongly triggers halt" risk but missed this inverse. (2) `claude-sonnet-4-6` returned the data agent's quality-check list wrapped in ` ```json … ``` ` markdown fences; the data-agent's JSON parser raised `Expecting value: line 1 column 1 (char 0): '```json\n[...'` and the agent graph crashed with `EXECUTION_FAILED`. Either the prompt forbids fences explicitly or the parser strips a fence before `json.loads`. Repro: the run-#1 invocation above.
- **Next:** plan critical path complete (Phases 1–3 shipped across Sessions 49/50/51). Two new BACKLOG items filed from the live round-trip (see above). Future session: pick any of 7 `[ ]` BACKLOG items, or optional plan Phase 4 (UI writes envelope) per plan §7.4.

### 2026-04-18 — Resume-from-checkpoint Phase 2: `run_pipeline` honors `resume_from` (Session 50)

Executes Phase 2 of `docs/planning/resume-from-checkpoint-plan.md` per plan §7.2. Wires `PipelineConfig.resume_from` into `run_pipeline`'s execution path: stages earlier than the resume point load their envelopes from disk via `CheckpointStore.load_payload`; the resume point and every stage after it re-executes. Halt semantics preserved — `FAILED_AT_*` only fires when the stage actually ran (plan §11 risk #5). `PipelineResult.resume_point` is populated on every return path (success, failure, or `already_complete` refusal) so operators can see whether a failed run originated inside a resumed invocation. Phase 3 (`--resume` CLI + OPERATIONS) is a separate session per plan §14.

- **Added:** `src/model_project_constructor/orchestrator/pipeline.py` — `PipelineConfig.resume_from: ResumePoint | None = None` (new field, default preserves pre-Phase-2 behavior); `PipelineResult.resume_point: ResumePoint | None = None` (propagated on every return path); load-or-execute branch logic in `run_pipeline` for the intake, adapter, and data stages; `ValueError` refusal when `resume_from == "already_complete"` (Phase 3 CLI will translate this to an operator-facing exit code); `typing.cast` used on `CheckpointStore.load_payload` results (the registry is the single source of truth for envelope-type mapping, zero runtime cost).
- **Added:** `TestRunPipelineResume` class in `tests/orchestrator/test_pipeline.py` — 6 tests: default-behavior regression under `resume_from=None`, S1/S2/S3 load-or-execute cases, `halt_under_resume_records_resume_point_on_failure` (plan §11 risk #5 regression), and `already_complete_raises_value_error`. The S2 case pins §6.3's "loaded request is ground truth" decision by seeding a tweaked `source_ref` on the disk envelope and asserting the data runner sees the loaded value, not a fresh adapter derivation.
- **Added:** `_seed_envelope` + `_make_resume_config` test helpers for resume-execution tests. `_seed_envelope` writes a real `HandoffEnvelope` (so `load_payload` can resolve the payload through the schema registry), distinct from `_touch_checkpoint` (for pure-function existence-only tests).
- **Verified:** `uv run pytest -q` → **461/461 passing** (was 455 pre-session; +6 new tests). Coverage **97.33%** with `Required test coverage of 95% reached`. `src/model_project_constructor/orchestrator/pipeline.py` → **100% coverage** on the 92 statements + 32 branches (up from 82 stmts + 24 branches pre-session — +10 stmts + 8 branches, all exercised). `uv run ruff check src/ tests/ packages/` → `All checks passed!`. `uv run mypy src/` → `Success: no issues found in 48 source files`.
- **Unchanged intentionally:** `scripts/run_pipeline.py` is untouched — Phase 3 adds the `--resume` CLI flag. `OrchestratorSettings` (`config.py`) untouched. LangGraph intra-agent checkpointers (`MemorySaver`, `SqliteSaver`) untouched per plan §10 anti-scope. BACKLOG "Automated resume-from-checkpoint" line stays open until plan's Phase 3 per §7.3.1.
- **Next:** Phase 3 of `resume-from-checkpoint-plan.md` (§7.3) — add `--resume <run_id>` argparse flag, rewrite `scripts/run_pipeline.py:main()` to resolve the resume point via `determine_resume_point` and construct `PipelineConfig` with the resolved value, OPERATIONS.md §5 rewrite, `docs/tutorial.md` §7, 4–5 new CLI tests, BACKLOG -2 lines, one live resume round-trip documented with its `run_id`.

### 2026-04-17 — Unclosed-SQLite `ResourceWarning` noise eliminated (Session 35)

Closes the BACKLOG "Fix unclosed SQLite connection warnings" item (prior count "~20 + 1"; Session 34 characterized the actual count as 26 running `uv run pytest -W default`; Session 35's pre-change tracemalloc pass measured 28 — the 2-warning delta is scheduling noise between runs, all of the same class). Two independent root causes, both in test-side lifecycle gaps rather than production defects:

- **Fixed (root cause A — 15 warnings):** `IntakeSessionStore._conn` (a raw `sqlite3.Connection` opened at `src/model_project_constructor/ui/intake/runner.py:84` with `check_same_thread=False` for the FastAPI threadpool) was never closed under test. `IntakeSessionStore.close()` exists and is wired into the FastAPI `lifespan` at `src/model_project_constructor/ui/intake/app.py:79-86`, but `starlette.testclient.TestClient` only fires `lifespan` when used as a context manager (`with TestClient(app) as client:`); `tests/ui/intake/conftest.py` instantiated `TestClient(make_app(fixture))` bare, so `lifespan` never ran. Fix in `conftest.py`: `make_app` is now a yielding fixture that tracks every `FastAPI` app it produces in a list and, on teardown, calls `app.state.store.close()` for each. `make_client`, `subrogation_client`, and the direct `make_client(...)` usages in `test_caps_and_revisions.py:31,:48` inherit the fix via the fixture dependency chain — no test body changes were needed.
- **Fixed (root cause B — 13 warnings):** `ReadOnlyDB._engine` (the SQLAlchemy engine assigned at `packages/data-agent/src/model_project_constructor_data_agent/db.py:37`) was never disposed, so the pooled sqlite3 connections lingered until Python 3.13's stricter GC surfaced them at arbitrary frames (`threading.py:281/303`, `ast.py:279`, `anyio/_backends/_asyncio.py:342`, `rich/protocol.py`, `yaml/reader.py`, etc. — the frames in the warning are NOT the allocation sites). Plus a sibling leak in `tests/agents/data/conftest.py:32` where the `seeded_sqlite_url` fixture's own setup engine was never disposed, and an inline engine in `tests/data_agent_package/test_cli.py:60` with the same gap.
- **Added:** `ReadOnlyDB.close()` method (`db.py:47-54`) — idempotent, safe to call without a prior `connect()`. Calls `self._engine.dispose()` and sets `_engine = None`. No context-manager (`__enter__/__exit__`) methods added — explicit `close()` via try/finally covers every current call site, and YAGNI rules out the extra surface until a caller needs it.
- **Changed:** `packages/data-agent/src/model_project_constructor_data_agent/cli.py:102-108` — wrapped `agent.run(...)` in a `try/finally` so `db.close()` fires even if the run raises. CLI is the owner of the `ReadOnlyDB` lifetime for the `--db-url` path; the agent and its graph treat `db` as borrowed.
- **Changed:** `tests/agents/data/conftest.py` — `seeded_sqlite_url` is now a yielding fixture with `engine.dispose()` in `finally`; `seeded_db` is now a yielding fixture with `db.close()` in `finally`. Both maintain `tmp_path` isolation; teardown order is correct (dependents tear down before dependencies per pytest fixture contract, so `seeded_db.close()` runs before `seeded_sqlite_url.engine.dispose()`).
- **Changed:** `tests/data_agent_package/test_cli.py:58-64` — wrapped the inline `engine.begin()` block in `try/finally` with `engine.dispose()`. One-liner; no test behavior change.
- **Verified:** `uv run pytest -W default --tb=no -q` now prints **0 warnings** (previously 25-28 of class `ResourceWarning: unclosed database`). `uv run pytest -q` 445/445 passing; coverage **97.27%** (up 0.01pp from 97.26% — the new `close()` statements count as covered because both test fixtures and the CLI `try/finally` exercise them). `uv run ruff check src/ tests/ packages/` clean. `uv run mypy src/ packages/` clean on 60 source files. `PYTHONTRACEMALLOC=25 uv run pytest -W default --tb=no -q` re-run after the fix: 0 `unclosed database` hits.
- **Unchanged (intentionally):** `ReadOnlyDB` has no `__enter__/__exit__` — see "No context-manager" note above. Also unchanged: `test_data_agent.py:211` (`unreachable = ReadOnlyDB("sqlite:////nonexistent...")`) — `ReadOnlyDB.connect()` fails inside the agent's node before `_engine` is ever assigned, so no engine leaks; calling `close()` there would be a no-op. Also unchanged: `src/model_project_constructor/agents/intake/graph.py` uses `MemorySaver()` (in-memory, no sqlite); `src/model_project_constructor/agents/website/graph.py` same. The only LangGraph SQLite saver usage is `SqliteSaver` in `ui/intake/runner.py`, which is covered by root cause A.

### 2026-04-17 — Self-hosted GitHub URL override wired through pipeline script (Session 30)

- **Fixed:** `scripts/run_pipeline.py:273-278` live GitHub branch had two related bugs that together meant the `--live --host github` path had never worked. (a) It passed `token=token` to `PyGithubAdapter(...)`, but the adapter's keyword-only parameter is `private_token=` (the kwarg-only signature at `src/model_project_constructor/agents/website/github_adapter.py:66-72`). First invocation would have raised `TypeError: __init__() got an unexpected keyword argument 'token'`. Parallels Session 22's `url=` → `host_url=` fix for the GitLab branch. (b) It never read `MPC_HOST_URL`, so even after the TypeError was noticed, GitHub Enterprise users would silently have hit public `api.github.com` despite `docs/tutorial.md §5c` claiming the env var works for GHE. Fix mirrors the GitLab branch directly below: read `MPC_HOST_URL` with `https://api.github.com` as the default, pass `private_token=token, host_url=host_url`.
- **Added:** 3 new tests in `tests/scripts/test_run_pipeline_adapter.py` (441 → 444; +3). `test_build_website_runner_github_live_threads_host_url` — with `MPC_HOST_URL=https://github.mycompany.com/api/v3`, `PyGithubAdapter` receives `private_token=<GITHUB_TOKEN>` + that exact `host_url`. `test_build_website_runner_github_live_defaults_host_url` — with `MPC_HOST_URL` unset, `host_url` defaults to `https://api.github.com`. `test_build_website_runner_gitlab_live_threads_host_url` — regression guard for the symmetric GitLab branch. All three monkeypatch the adapter classes + `WebsiteAgent` so no live HTTP is made. Pattern follows the existing `importlib.util` module-loading approach since `scripts/` is not a package.
- **Verified:** `uv run pytest -q` 444/444 passing, coverage 97.26%; `uv run ruff check src/ tests/ packages/ scripts/` clean; `uv run mypy` clean on all 60 source files. No adapter change required — `PyGithubAdapter.__init__` already accepted `host_url=` (and already wired it into `Github(auth=..., base_url=host_url)` at `github_adapter.py:72`); the gap was purely in the pipeline script's call site. Not live-tested against a real GitHub Enterprise (no test instance); parallels Session 22's GitLab `host_url=` fix which was also shipped on code-read + unit-test coverage only. Closes the last of Session 22's three code-gap findings (validator + ruff-scope + mypy-scope already landed in Sessions 28/25/29).

### 2026-04-17 — CI mypy coverage extended to `packages/` (Session 29)

- **Changed:** `.github/workflows/ci.yml` typecheck job now runs `uv run mypy` (no explicit path), which picks up the `[tool.mypy] packages = ["model_project_constructor", "model_project_constructor_data_agent"]` declaration in `pyproject.toml`. Previously CI ran `mypy src/` only, so pre-existing errors in `packages/data-agent/` never blocked merges.   - `anthropic_client.py:218` — 11 × `union-attr` on `response.content[0].text`. The Anthropic SDK's `ContentBlock` is a 13-variant union; only `TextBlock` has `.text`. Fix: added `from anthropic.types import TextBlock` + `isinstance(block, TextBlock)` guard that raises `LLMParseError` with the actual class name if the first block is not a `TextBlock`. Production behavior is unchanged on the happy path (Claude always returns `TextBlock` for our `messages=[{"role": "user", ...}]` payload); the guard replaces an implicit `AttributeError` with a typed error that the Data Agent's outer try/except already handles.
  - `nodes.py:142` — `arg-type` on `execution_status=status` because `status = "PASSED" if rows else "FAILED"` inferred as `str` not `Literal["PASSED", "FAILED"]`. Fix: explicit `status: Literal["PASSED", "FAILED"] = ...` annotation + `Literal` import.
  - `sql_validation.py:26` — `no-untyped-call` on `parsed[0].get_type()` (sqlparse is untyped in mypy's strict mode). Fix: inline `# type: ignore[no-untyped-call]` — minimal, localized, accurate (3rd-party untyped API).
- **Changed:** `tests/data_agent_package/test_anthropic_client.py` — replaced the `_FakeResponse` dataclass (duck-typed `.text` attr) with real `anthropic.types.TextBlock(text=..., type="text")` instances in `_FakeMessages.create`. Tests now exercise the production `isinstance(block, TextBlock)` guard faithfully.
- **Added:** `test_call_claude_rejects_non_text_block` in `tests/data_agent_package/test_anthropic_client.py` — covers the new defensive branch that raises `LLMParseError("expected TextBlock...")` when the first content block is not a `TextBlock`. 440 → 441 tests.
- **Verified:** `uv run pytest -q` 441/441 passing, coverage 97.26%; `uv run ruff check src/ tests/ packages/ scripts/` clean; `uv run mypy` clean on all 60 source files (`src/` + `packages/data-agent/src/`).

### 2026-04-16 — First live end-to-end smoke test (Session 22, Scope A)

- **Fixed:** `scripts/run_pipeline.py:119` passed `url=` to `PythonGitLabAdapter(...)` but the constructor's keyword-only parameter is `host_url=`. `--live --host gitlab` had never successfully run — it would have raised `TypeError: __init__() got an unexpected keyword argument 'url'` on the first keystroke. Phase 4B/5 tests only exercise the adapter's import + protocol conformance, not the script's wiring. Two-character fix: `url=host_url` → `host_url=host_url`.
- **Verified:** after the fix, `uv run python scripts/run_pipeline.py --live --host gitlab --run-id run_live_002` created a real project at `https://gitlab.com/rmsharp-modelpilot/subrogation-pilot` (project ID 81385820, initial commit `3dec542`). Website stage latency: 3,501 ms live vs ~4 ms fake-mode (~875× expected network overhead). All 38 files present, all 10 tier-3-moderate governance artifacts created, regulatory mapping correct (SR_11_7 + NAIC_AIS).
- **Findings (filed in `BACKLOG.md`, unfixed in this session):**
  - `MPC_NAMESPACE` takes a group *path* (`rmsharp-modelpilot`), not a URL (`https://gitlab.com/rmsharp-modelpilot`). `.env.example`, `OPERATIONS.md` §1, and `docs/tutorial.md` §5 all omit this constraint. The GitLab adapter returns a generic `"group lookup failed: 404"` instead of a clearer `"MPC_NAMESPACE must be a group path, not a URL"`.
  - `scripts/` is not ruff-checked in CI (`.github/workflows/ci.yml` lint job scans `src/ tests/ packages/` only). Running ruff locally surfaces 10 `E402` errors in `scripts/run_pipeline.py` — the `sys.path.insert` pattern needs `# noqa: E402` or a refactor.
  - `packages/` is not mypy-checked in CI (typecheck job runs `mypy src/` only) even though `[tool.mypy] packages = [...]` in `pyproject.toml` declares both `model_project_constructor` and `model_project_constructor_data_agent`. Running mypy with the declared packages surfaces 13 errors, mostly in `packages/data-agent/.../anthropic_client.py:218` — Anthropic SDK's content-block union has grown ~8 variants since the code was written, and `block.text` no longer narrows without a type guard. Latent bit-rot.
  - `docs/tutorial.md` §5c says `MPC_HOST_URL="https://github.mycompany.com/api/v3"` works for GitHub Enterprise, but `scripts/run_pipeline.py:109-113` constructs `PyGithubAdapter(token=token)` with no URL argument — GHE users silently hit public `api.github.com`. (Not live-tested in Session 22; confirmed by code reading.)
  - `docs/tutorial.md` §5 and `OPERATIONS.md` §4.2/4.3 document two different live-run commands. The OPERATIONS recipe uses `python -m model_project_constructor.agents.website --intake ... --host ... --private-token ...` — this flow should be reconciled with the script path or marked deprecated.

## [0.1.0 — Pilot Ready] — 2026-04-15

Phases 1 through 6 complete. 422 tests at 97.18% coverage. Both GitLab and GitHub adapters pass structural + integration tests. CI green across lint, typecheck, test, and decoupling jobs.

### Pilot readiness audit (Session 17) — 2026-04-15

- **Added:** `docs/planning/pilot-readiness-audit.md` — all 46 `architecture-plan.md §14` acceptance criteria verified against the actual codebase. PILOT-READY declared. Commit: `b9c87c7`.
- **Fixed:** all 62 pre-existing ruff errors across `src/`, `tests/`, `packages/` (56 auto-fixable + 6 manual). Commit: `17f661d`.
- **Fixed:** 3 CI workflow failures — mypy typecheck missing `--extra ui`, decoupling job below coverage floor, CLI tests failing on ANSI color codes (resolved via `click.unstyle`). Commits: `d62efc2`, `b8d8d7e`.
- **Changed:** `BACKLOG.md` marks all 6 phases complete. Commit: `66b44c8`.

### Phase 6 — Production hardening (Session 16) — 2026-04-15

- **Added:** structured logging via `make_logged_runner` — binds `run_id` and `correlation_id` to every stage's log context; emits `agent.start` / `agent.end` / `agent.error` events.
- **Added:** in-memory metrics via `MetricsRegistry` + `make_measured_runner` — run counts, status distribution, per-agent latency; no external metrics backend required.
- **Added:** `OrchestratorSettings.from_env()` — env-var-driven configuration with `require_*` guards that fail fast on missing credentials; `.env.example` template.
- **Added:** `.github/workflows/ci.yml` — four-job CI pipeline (`lint`, `typecheck`, `test`, `decoupling`).
- **Added:** `OPERATIONS.md` production runbook and `TROUBLESHOOTING.md` diagnostics (one walkthrough per `FAILED_AT_*` path).
- **Added:** 54 new tests across 3 new test files.
- **Design:** zero new dependencies. Observability is opt-in via runner composition — `pipeline.py` has no imports from the new logging/metrics modules.
- **Changed:** coverage floor raised 93% → 94%. Commit: `c3943a8`.
- Feature commit: `2060d4a`.

- **Added:** `src/model_project_constructor/orchestrator/` package — `pipeline.py` (sequential `run_pipeline` driver per §12), `adapters.py` (sole `IntakeReport ↔ DataRequest` bridge enforcing the §7 decoupling invariant), `checkpoints.py` (`CheckpointStore` with envelope save/load + `save_result` for terminal artifacts).
- **Added:** `PipelineStatus` literal — `COMPLETE` / `FAILED_AT_INTAKE` / `FAILED_AT_DATA` / `FAILED_AT_WEBSITE`. Partial state retained on halt so operators can inspect or resume.
- **Added:** 45 new tests under `tests/orchestrator/` — happy path for both `ci_platform="gitlab"` and `ci_platform="github"` with positive + negative CI-file assertions; halt behavior for each `FAILED_AT_*` path; downstream-agent-not-called guards; adapter inference rules; envelope round-trip; terminal-result filename isolation; run-directory isolation.
